- **handoff**: Cross-session handoff for the orchestration tooling — the heavier alternative to /compact. ([adithyanraj03/dsh-skills-pack](https://github.com/adithyanraj03/dsh-skills-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/handoff))
- **handoff**: Manual command that carries work across a gap, in three modes. Default: compacts the current conversation into a handoff document a fresh session can pick up, and with --bg launches that session as a background agent. --catchup: rebuilds a returning human's context from live project state, read-only. --recover: recovers a session that died without a handoff, from a transcript digest. Not for restoring a conversation whole (claude --resume). ([Adminhead/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Adminhead/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/handoff/skills/handoff))
- **handoff**: Compact this conversation into a handoff document for the next session; optionally spawn it. ([adnancrnovrsanin/lucy](https://github.com/adnancrnovrsanin/lucy/tree/HEAD/skills/handoff))
- **handoff**: Use when writing any handoff or task brief, including an inline brief for Codex or another agent that the user will paste themselves. An inline-only, read-only, or do-not-launch request still needs this skill to preserve the acceptance checklist and return contract. Also use when ending a session, approaching compaction, switching context, resuming a prior handoff, recovering degraded context, or delegating to native subagents. Triggers include "prepare an inline handoff", "handoff this to Codex", "brief a subagent", "handoff", "resume", and "reground". - **handoff**: Create a compact, artifact-first transfer of the current task for another agent or a fresh task. ([Aethergrids/Metis](https://github.com/Aethergrids/Metis/tree/HEAD/plugins/metis-prelude/skills/handoff))
